❯ You are an expert Lean 4 developer. Your goal is to resolve as many sorry     
  statements as possible in this directory.                                     
                                                                                
  Because Lean files have strict import dependencies, you MUST follow this      
  execution plan:                                                               
                                                                                
  1. Map Dependencies:                                                          
  Scan all .lean files and their import statements.                             
  Build a dependency graph to determine the correct bottom-up compilation       
  order.                                                                        
                                                                                
  2. Proactive Annotation & Knowledge Base                                      
                                                                                
  Before proving, analyze all existing lemmas in the file. Add appropriate Lean 
   4 attributes—such as @[simp], @[aesop safe/unsafe], @[ext], @[refl], or      
  @[grind =]—to existing declarations to optimize the automation                
  environment.-Consult the Mathlib 4 Tactic Documentation                       
  https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/tactics.html for precise 
   syntax and to identify the most effective attributes for specific logical    
  structures.                                                                   
                                                                                
  3. Best-Effort Processing:                                                    
  Process files one by one, starting from the leaf dependencies.                
  For each file, use the Aristotle MCP  to attempt closing all gaps.            
                                                                                
  4. Reference & Assist:                                                        
  If Aristotle fails on a specific goal, consult Mathlib 4 Tactic Documentation 
   and apply basic structural tactics (intro, cases, induction) to break the    
  goal down, then hand it back to Aristotle.                                    
                                                                                
  5. Graceful Fallback (Do Not Get Stuck):                                      
  If a sorry remains unsolvable after your assistance, leave the sorry in place 
   and move on. Do not waste excessive loops on a single stubborn theorem.      
                                                                                
  6. Validation:                                                                
  Run lake build on the current module.                                         
  You may proceed to the next file in the dependency graph if the current file  
  compiles without fatal errors, and you had tried your best to prove the       
  theorems in the current file.

      385 +        rw [hL1] at h                                         
      386 +        cases hL2 : ALOOKUP e2 k1 with                        
      387 +        | none => rw [hL2] at h; exact h.elim                 
      388 +        | some v2' =>                                         
      389 +          rw [hL2] at h                                       
      390 +          obtain ⟨hR, hrest⟩ := h                             
      391 +          rcases hk with ⟨hkeq⟩ | hkeq                        
      392 +          · rename_i hkeq                                     
      393 +            have : k1 = k_ := LawfulBEq.eq_of_beq hkeq        
      394 +            subst this                                        
      395 +            exact ⟨v1', v2', hL1, hL2, hR⟩                    
      396 +          · rw [ih] at hrest                                  
      397 +            exact hrest k_ hkeq                               
      398 +    · intro h                                                 
      399 +      have hh := h k1 (by simp [MEM])                         
      400 +      obtain ⟨v1', v2', hL1, hL2, hR⟩ := hh                   
      401 +      rw [hL1, hL2]                                           
      402 +      refine ⟨hR, ?_⟩                                         
      403 +      rw [ih]                                                 
      404 +      intro k_ hk                                             
      405 +      exact h k_ (by simp [MEM]; exact Or.inr (by simp [MEM] a
          +t hk; exact hk))

      458 +        rw [hT23, hT12]                                       
      459 +      · -- FLOOKUP s2 l = none. But s2 = FUNION t12 s1, so bot

      467  Theorem store_assign_type_sound:

      319  theorem nsAll_nsAppend_left {m n v : Type} [BEq m] [BEq n] :
      320      ∀ (P : cml_id m n → v → Prop) (n1 n2 : «namespace» m n v),

      328 +  | Bind v2 m2 =>

          +(Bind (v1++v2) (m1++m2)) (Short x) = some val_

      328  theorem disjoint_env_weakCT :
      329      ∀ (cm cm' : ctMap),
      330        Set.Disjoint (Finmap.FDOM cm') (Finmap.FDOM cm) →
      331 -      weakCT (Finmap.FUNION cm' cm) cm := sorry               
      331 +      weakCT (Finmap.FUNION cm' cm) cm := by                  
      332 +  intros cm cm' h                                             
      333 +  intro k val_ hk                                             
      334 +  -- Goal: FLOOKUP (FUNION cm' cm) k = some val_              
      335 +  -- We have: hk : FLOOKUP cm k = some val_                   
      336 +  show Finmap.FUNION cm' cm k = some val_                     
      337 +  unfold Finmap.FUNION                                        
      338 +  -- Either cm' k = none (and then result is cm k = some val_)
      339 +  -- Or cm' k = some v', but then by disjointness, cm k = none
          +, contradicting hk                                            
      340 +  cases h' : cm' k with                                       
      341 +  | none => simp [h']; exact hk                               
      342 +  | some v' =>                                                
      343 +    exfalso                                                   
      344 +    have h1 : k ∈ Finmap.FDOM cm' := by show (cm' k).isSome = 
          +true; rw [h']; rfl                                            
      345 +    have h2 : k ∈ Finmap.FDOM cm := by                        
      346 +      show (cm k).isSome = true                               
      347 +      show Finmap.FLOOKUP cm k = some val_ at hk              
      348 +      unfold Finmap.FLOOKUP at hk                             
      349 +      rw [hk]; rfl                                            
      350 +    exact h k ⟨h1, h2⟩

      352  Theorem type_tenv_ctor_weakening:
